:
Modulus Drop (
ModDrop
)

Encoding and Decoding the Plaintext

TFHE

No need, because each plaintext is a single number

BFV

Must convert the input slots into polynomial coefficients to support batch processing:

CKKS

- Encoding input slots
\vec{v}
into polynomial coefficients:
\vec{m}=n^{-1}\cdot\vec{v}\cdot I_{n}^{R}\cdot\tilde{W}

BGV

- Decoding polynomial coefficients
\vec{m}
into input slots:
\vec{v}=\vec{m}\cdot\tilde{W}^{*}

Table 25
:
Encoding and Decoding the Plaintext

Input Slot Rotation

TFHE

Not applicable, because its plaintext is a single number (i.e., a single slot)

BFV

Given
\textsf{ct}=\textsf{RLWE}_{S(X),\sigma}\bm{(}\Delta M(X)\bm{)}=\bm{(}A(X),B(X)\bm{)}
,

to rotate the input slots by
h
positions to the left:

CKKS

1. Update
ct
to
\textsf{RLWE}_{S(X^{J(h)}),\sigma}\bm{(}\Delta M(X^{J(h)})\bm{)}=\bm{(}A(X^{J(h)})
,
B(X^{J(h)})\bm{)}

(where
J(h)=5^{h}\bmod 2n
)

2. Key-switch
\textsf{RLWE}_{S(X^{J(h)}),\sigma}\bm{(}\Delta M(X^{J(h)})\bm{)}
to
\textsf{RLWE}_{S(X),\sigma}\bm{(}\Delta M(X^{J(h)})\bm{)}
.

BGV

Given
\textsf{ct}=\textsf{RLWE}_{S(X),\sigma}\bm{(}M(X)\bm{)}=\bm{(}A(X),B(X)\bm{)}
,

to rotate the input slots by
h
positions to the left:

1. Update
ct
to
\textsf{RLWE}_{S(X^{J(h)}),\sigma}\bm{(}M(X^{J(h)})\bm{)}=\bm{(}A(X^{J(h)})
,
B(X^{J(h)})\bm{)}

2. Key-switch
\textsf{RLWE}_{S(X^{J(h)}),\sigma}\bm{(}M(X^{J(h)})\bm{)}
to
\textsf{RLWE}_{S(X),\sigma}\bm{(}M(X^{J(h)})\bm{)}
.

Table 26
:
Input Slot Rotation

Bootstrapping Goal

TFHE

To reset the noise.

BFV

CKKS

To reset the ciphertext modulus from
q_{0}\rightarrow q_{L}
(technically, to
q_{l^{\prime}}
where
q_{0}<q_{l^{\prime}}<q_{L}
).

BGV

Table 27
:
Bootstrapping Goal

Bootstrapping Details

TFHE

1.
Modulus Switch
from
q\rightarrow 2n
to convert
\textsf{LWE}_{\vec{s},\sigma}(\Delta m)\rightarrow\textsf{LWE}_{\vec{s},\sigma}(\hat{\Delta}m)=(\vec{\hat{a}},\hat{b})\bmod 2n
,

where
\hat{\Delta}=\dfrac{2n}{t}
.

\rhd
where
t
divides
2n

2.
Blind Rotation
: Homomorphically rotate the GLWE-encrypted look-up table

polynomial
\textsf{GLWE}_{\vec{S},\sigma}(\Delta V)
by
\Delta m+e
positions to the left. This is done by

by homomorphically deriving
\textsf{GLWE}_{\vec{S},\sigma}(\Delta V_{k})
as follows:

\textsf{GLWE}_{\vec{S},\sigma}(\Delta V_{0})=\textsf{GLWE}_{\vec{S},\sigma}(\Delta V)\cdot X^{-\hat{b}}

\textsf{GLWE}_{\vec{S},\sigma}(\Delta V_{i})=\textsf{GLWE}_{\vec{S},\sigma}(\Delta V_{i-1})\cdot X^{\hat{a}_{i}s_{i-1}}

=\textsf{GGSW}_{\vec{S},\sigma}^{\beta,l}(s_{i-1})\cdot\bm{(}\textsf{GLWE}_{\vec{S},\sigma}(\Delta V_{i-1})\cdot X^{\hat{a}_{i-1}}-\textsf{GLWE}_{\vec{S},\sigma}(\Delta V_{i-1})\bm{)}+\textsf{GLWE}_{\vec{S},\sigma}(\Delta V_{i-1})

3.
Coefficient Extraction
: The rotated encrypted polynomial
V_{k}
’s constant term value is

\Delta m
. Extract this encrypted constant term as
\textsf{LWE}_{\vec{s},\sigma}(\Delta m)
from
\textsf{GLWE}_{\vec{S},\sigma}(\Delta V_{k})
.

BFV

1.
Modulus Switch
from
q\rightarrow p^{\varepsilon}
to convert
\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(\Delta M)\rightarrow\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(p^{\varepsilon-1}M)\bmod p^{\varepsilon}

2.
Homomorphic Decryption
:

B+A\cdot\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(\Delta^{\prime}S)=\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(\Delta^{\prime}\cdot(p^{\varepsilon-1}M+E+Kp^{\varepsilon}))\bmod q
, where
\Delta^{\prime}=\left\lfloor\dfrac{q}{p^{\varepsilon}}\right\rfloor

3.
CoeffToSlot
: Multiply to the ciphertext by
n^{-1}\cdot I_{n}^{R}\cdot\tilde{W}
to move

the plaintext coefficients of
p^{\varepsilon-1}M+E+Kp^{\varepsilon}
to the input slots.

4.
Digit Extraction
: Given the digit extraction polynomial
G_{\varepsilon}(x)
,

homomorphically compute:

G_{2}\circ G_{3}\circ\cdots\circ G_{\varepsilon-1}(p^{\varepsilon-1}M+E+Kp^{\varepsilon})

, and then the output
M+K^{\langle\varepsilon-1\rangle}p
is stored in the plaintext slots.

Use scaling factor re-interpretation to handle inverse-
p
multiplications.

5.
SlotToCoeff
: Multiply to the ciphertext by
\tilde{W}^{*}
to move
M+K^{\langle\varepsilon-1\rangle}p
to the

polynomial coefficient positions and get
\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(\Delta^{\prime}\cdot(M+K^{\langle\varepsilon-1\rangle}p))\bmod q

=\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(\Delta M)\bmod q

Table 28
:
Bootstrapping Details: TFHE, BFV

Bootstrapping Details

CKKS

1.
ModRaise
:
View the ciphertext
(A,B)\bmod q_{0}
as
(A,B)\bmod q_{L}

2.
CoeffToSlot
: Move the coefficients of
\Delta M+E+Kq_{0}
to the input slots.

3.
EvalExp
: Homomorphically evaluate the polynomial approximation of

the sine function with period
q_{0}
at
\Delta M+E+Kq_{0}
,

which outputs an encryption of
\Delta M+E
in the plaintext slots.

4.
SlotToCoeff
: Move
\Delta M+E
to the polynomial coefficient positions

to get
\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(\Delta M+E)
.

BGV

1.
Modulus Switch
from
q_{l}\rightarrow\hat{q}
to convert

\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(M)=(A,B)\bmod q_{l}\rightarrow\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(M)=(\hat{A},\hat{B})\bmod\hat{q}

, where
\hat{q}\equiv 1\bmod p^{\varepsilon}

2.
Ciphertext Coefficient Multiplication by
p^{\varepsilon-1}
:

Compute
(p^{\varepsilon-1}\hat{A},p^{\varepsilon-1}\hat{B})=(A^{\prime},B^{\prime})\bmod\hat{q}
(where
\hat{q}\equiv 1\bmod p^{\varepsilon}
)

, which the ciphertext
\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(p^{\varepsilon-1}M)\bmod\hat{q}
with noise
p^{\varepsilon}E
.

3.
ModRaise
:
(A^{\prime},B^{\prime})\bmod\hat{q}\rightarrow(A^{\prime},B^{\prime})\bmod q_{L}

, which is the ciphertext
\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(p^{\varepsilon-1}M+p^{\varepsilon}E+K^{\prime}\hat{q})\bmod q_{L}
.

4.
CoeffToSlot
: Multiply to the ciphertext by
n^{-1}\cdot I_{n}^{R}\cdot\tilde{W}
to move

the plaintext coefficients of
p^{\varepsilon-1}M+p^{\varepsilon}E+K^{\prime}\hat{q}
to the input slots.

5.
Digit Extraction
: Given the digit extraction polynomial
G_{\varepsilon}(x)
,

homomorphically compute:

\bm{(}\text{ }G_{2}\circ G_{3}\circ\cdots\circ G_{\varepsilon}(p^{\varepsilon-1}M+p^{\varepsilon}E+K^{\prime}\hat{q})\text{ }\bm{)}

, and then the output
M+K^{\prime\prime}p
is stored in the plaintext slots.

To handle inverse-
p
multiplication in each
i
-th round, multiply
|p^{-1}|_{q^{\langle i\rangle}}
to all

ciphertext polynomial coefficients to update their plaintext portion from

Mp^{\varepsilon-i}+K^{\prime\prime\prime}p^{\varepsilon-i+1}\pmod{q^{\langle i\rangle}}
to
M^{\varepsilon-i-1}+K^{\langle i\rangle}p^{\varepsilon-i}\pmod{q^{\langle i\rangle}}

\rhd
where
q^{\langle i\rangle}
is the ciphertext modulus at each specific round

6.
SlotToCoeff
: Multiply to the ciphertext by
\tilde{W}^{*}
to move
M+K^{\langle\varepsilon-1\rangle}p
to the

polynomial coefficient positions to get
\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(M+K^{\langle\textit{last}\rangle}p)

=\textsf{RLWE}_{S,\sigma}(M+\Delta K^{\langle\textit{last}\rangle})\bmod q_{l^{\prime}}

\rhd
where
\Delta=p
, and the final noise is
K^{\langle\textit{last}\rangle}

Table 29
:
Bootstrapping Details: CKKS, BGV

Noise Management

TFHE

Their bootstrapping resets the noise.

BFV

CKKS

- The noise grows without stopping, because its bootstrapping resets only the

modulus chain. To slow down the noise growth, we should increase the plaintext’s

scaling factor
\Delta
.

- CKKS’s
EvalExp
cannot use the digit extraction polynomial to remove the noise,

because CKKS’s plaintext is not in a modulus ring, but is a real number.

BGV

BGV’s modulus switch has the special property of resetting the noise, and BGV’s

bootstrapping resets the modulus chain to enable indefinite modulus switches.

Table 30
:
Noise Management

Python Demo FHE Library

We provide our Python Demo FHE library (TFHE, BFV, CKKS, BGV) for educational purposes. The source code and the manual are available at
https://github.com/fhetextbook/fhe-textbook
.
